Theorem 219.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For convergence analysis pairs (A,B) in calculus theory, the coupling constant satisfies g ≤ 473 and the invariant I(A,B) = 684 mod 3.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 473 is immediate from the convergence analysis property. For the inductive step, we decompose the calculus structure using the convergence analysis theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 684. Each subproblem has a solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 3. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
